<span style="display:none;" id="data/interdomain_rotation/APO/1/plumed.datCcom">The COM action with label <b>Ccom</b> calculates something</span><b name="data/interdomain_rotation/APO/1/plumed.dattwist" onclick='showPath("data/interdomain_rotation/APO/1/plumed.dat","data/interdomain_rotation/APO/1/plumed.dattwist","data/interdomain_rotation/APO/1/plumed.dattwist","brown")'>twist</b>: <span class="plumedtooltip" style="color:green">TORSION<span class="right">Calculate one or multiple torsional angles. <a href="https://www.plumed.org/doc-master/user-doc/html/TORSION" style="color:green">More details</a><i></i></span></span> <span class="plumedtooltip">VECTOR1<span class="right">You should use VECTORA instead of this keyword which was used in older versions of PLUMED and is provided for back compatibility only<i></i></span></span>=<b name="data/interdomain_rotation/APO/1/plumed.datN1">N1</b>,<b name="data/interdomain_rotation/APO/1/plumed.datN2">N2</b> <span class="plumedtooltip">AXIS<span class="right">two atoms that define an axis<i></i></span></span>=<b name="data/interdomain_rotation/APO/1/plumed.datNcom">Ncom</b>,<b name="data/interdomain_rotation/APO/1/plumed.datCcom">Ccom</b> <span class="plumedtooltip">VECTOR2<span class="right">You should use VECTORB instead of this keyword which was used in older versions of PLUMED and is provided for back compatibility only<i></i></span></span>=<b name="data/interdomain_rotation/APO/1/plumed.datC1">C1</b>,<b name="data/interdomain_rotation/APO/1/plumed.datC2">C2</b> <span class="plumedtooltip">NOPBC<span class="right"> ignore the periodic boundary conditions when calculating distances<i></i></span></span>
<br/><span style="color:blue" class="comment">#  RMSD / path CV </span>
<span style="display:none;" id="data/interdomain_rotation/APO/1/plumed.dattwist">The TORSION action with label <b>twist</b> calculates the following quantities:<table  align="center" frame="void" width="95%" cellpadding="5%"><tr><td width="5%"><b> Quantity </b>  </td><td><b> Description </b> </td></tr><tr><td width="5%">twist.value</td><td>the TORSION involving these atoms</td></tr></table></span><b name="data/interdomain_rotation/APO/1/plumed.datr_active_sq" onclick='showPath("data/interdomain_rotation/APO/1/plumed.dat","data/interdomain_rotation/APO/1/plumed.datr_active_sq","data/interdomain_rotation/APO/1/plumed.datr_active_sq","brown")'>r_active_sq</b>:   <span class="plumedtooltip" style="color:green">RMSD<span class="right">Calculate the RMSD with respect to a reference structure. <a href="https://www.plumed.org/doc-master/user-doc/html/RMSD" style="color:green">More details</a><i></i></span></span> <span class="plumedtooltip">REFERENCE<span class="right">a file in pdb format containing the reference structure and the atoms involved in the CV<i></i></span></span>=<b name="data/interdomain_rotation/APO/1/plumed.dat">../refs/holo_ndomain_align_cdomain_structured_ca.pdb</b> <span class="plumedtooltip">TYPE<span class="right"> the manner in which RMSD alignment is performed<i></i></span></span>=OPTIMAL <span class="plumedtooltip">SQUARED<span class="right">  This should be set if you want MSD instead of RMSD <i></i></span></span>
<span style="display:none;" id="data/interdomain_rotation/APO/1/plumed.datr_active_sq">The RMSD action with label <b>r_active_sq</b> calculates the following quantities:<table  align="center" frame="void" width="95%" cellpadding="5%"><tr><td width="5%"><b> Quantity </b>  </td><td><b> Description </b> </td></tr><tr><td width="5%">r_active_sq.value</td><td>the RMSD distance between the instantaneous structure and the reference structure/s that were input</td></tr></table></span><b name="data/interdomain_rotation/APO/1/plumed.datr_inactive_sq" onclick='showPath("data/interdomain_rotation/APO/1/plumed.dat","data/interdomain_rotation/APO/1/plumed.datr_inactive_sq","data/interdomain_rotation/APO/1/plumed.datr_inactive_sq","brown")'>r_inactive_sq</b>: <span class="plumedtooltip" style="color:green">RMSD<span class="right">Calculate the RMSD with respect to a reference structure. <a href="https://www.plumed.org/doc-master/user-doc/html/RMSD" style="color:green">More details</a><i></i></span></span> <span class="plumedtooltip">REFERENCE<span class="right">a file in pdb format containing the reference structure and the atoms involved in the CV<i></i></span></span>=<b name="data/interdomain_rotation/APO/1/plumed.dat">../refs/apo_ndomain_align_cdomain_structured_ca.pdb</b>  <span class="plumedtooltip">TYPE<span class="right"> the manner in which RMSD alignment is performed<i></i></span></span>=OPTIMAL <span class="plumedtooltip">SQUARED<span class="right">  This should be set if you want MSD instead of RMSD <i></i></span></span>
<span style="display:none;" id="data/interdomain_rotation/APO/1/plumed.datr_inactive_sq">The RMSD action with label <b>r_inactive_sq</b> calculates the following quantities:<table  align="center" frame="void" width="95%" cellpadding="5%"><tr><td width="5%"><b> Quantity </b>  </td><td><b> Description </b> </td></tr><tr><td width="5%">r_inactive_sq.value</td><td>the RMSD distance between the instantaneous structure and the reference structure/s that were input</td></tr></table></span><b name="data/interdomain_rotation/APO/1/plumed.datp_rmsd_twist_l0p1" onclick='showPath("data/interdomain_rotation/APO/1/plumed.dat","data/interdomain_rotation/APO/1/plumed.datp_rmsd_twist_l0p1","data/interdomain_rotation/APO/1/plumed.datp_rmsd_twist_l0p1","brown")'>p_rmsd_twist_l0p1</b>: <span class="plumedtooltip" style="color:green">FUNCPATHMSD<span class="right">This function calculates path collective variables. <a href="https://www.plumed.org/doc-master/user-doc/html/FUNCPATHMSD" style="color:green">More details</a><i></i></span></span> <span class="plumedtooltip">ARG<span class="right">the labels of the values from which the function is calculated<i></i></span></span>=<b name="data/interdomain_rotation/APO/1/plumed.datr_inactive_sq">r_inactive_sq</b>,<b name="data/interdomain_rotation/APO/1/plumed.datr_active_sq">r_active_sq</b> <span class="plumedtooltip">LAMBDA<span class="right">the lambda parameter is needed for smoothing, is in the units of plumed<i></i></span></span>=0.1

<span style="color:blue" class="comment">#  theta_rev (uses Ncom/Ccom; anchor from R62+R76 SC; FL midpoint 69/70 CA) </span>

<span style="display:none;" id="data/interdomain_rotation/APO/1/plumed.datCOM_R62_R76">The COM action with label <b>COM_R62_R76</b> calculates something</span><b name="data/interdomain_rotation/APO/1/plumed.datFL_M" onclick='showPath("data/interdomain_rotation/APO/1/plumed.dat","data/interdomain_rotation/APO/1/plumed.datFL_M","data/interdomain_rotation/APO/1/plumed.datFL_M","brown")'>FL_M</b>: <span class="plumedtooltip" style="color:green">GROUP<span class="right">Define a group of atoms so that a particular list of atoms can be referenced with a single label in definitions of CVs or virtual atoms. <a href="https://www.plumed.org/doc-master/user-doc/html/GROUP" style="color:green">More details</a><i></i></span></span> <span class="plumedtooltip">ATOMS<span class="right">the numerical indexes for the set of atoms in the group<i></i></span></span>=1108,1120

<span style="display:none;" id="data/interdomain_rotation/APO/1/plumed.datCOM_FL_M">The COM action with label <b>COM_FL_M</b> calculates something</span><b name="data/interdomain_rotation/APO/1/plumed.dattheta_rev" onclick='showPath("data/interdomain_rotation/APO/1/plumed.dat","data/interdomain_rotation/APO/1/plumed.dattheta_rev","data/interdomain_rotation/APO/1/plumed.dattheta_rev","brown")'>theta_rev</b>: <span class="plumedtooltip" style="color:green">ANGLE<span class="right">Calculate one or multiple angle/s. <a href="https://www.plumed.org/doc-master/user-doc/html/ANGLE" style="color:green">More details</a><i></i></span></span> <span class="plumedtooltip">ATOMS<span class="right">the list of atoms involved in this collective variable (either 3 or 4 atoms)<i></i></span></span>=<b name="data/interdomain_rotation/APO/1/plumed.datNcom">Ncom</b>,<b name="data/interdomain_rotation/APO/1/plumed.datCcom">Ccom</b>,<b name="data/interdomain_rotation/APO/1/plumed.datCOM_R62_R76">COM_R62_R76</b>,<b name="data/interdomain_rotation/APO/1/plumed.datCOM_FL_M">COM_FL_M</b> <span class="plumedtooltip">NOPBC<span class="right"> ignore the periodic boundary conditions when calculating distances<i></i></span></span>
<span style="display:none;" id="data/interdomain_rotation/APO/1/plumed.dattheta_rev">The ANGLE action with label <b>theta_rev</b> calculates the following quantities:<table  align="center" frame="void" width="95%" cellpadding="5%"><tr><td width="5%"><b> Quantity </b>  </td><td><b> Description </b> </td></tr><tr><td width="5%">theta_rev.value</td><td>the ANGLE involving these atoms</td></tr></table></span><b name="data/interdomain_rotation/APO/1/plumed.dattheta_rev_deg" onclick='showPath("data/interdomain_rotation/APO/1/plumed.dat","data/interdomain_rotation/APO/1/plumed.dattheta_rev_deg","data/interdomain_rotation/APO/1/plumed.dattheta_rev_deg","brown")'>theta_rev_deg</b>: <span class="plumedtooltip" style="color:green">MATHEVAL<span class="right">An alias to the CUSTOM function that can also be used to calaculate combinations of variables using a custom expression. <a href="https://www.plumed.org/doc-master/user-doc/html/MATHEVAL" style="color:green">More details</a><i></i></span></span> <span class="plumedtooltip">ARG<span class="right">the values input to this function<i></i></span></span>=<b name="data/interdomain_rotation/APO/1/plumed.dattheta_rev">theta_rev</b> <span class="plumedtooltip">FUNC<span class="right">the function you wish to evaluate<i></i></span></span>=x*180/pi <span class="plumedtooltip">PERIODIC<span class="right">if the output of your function is periodic then you should specify the periodicity of the function<i></i></span></span>=NO

<span style="display:none;" id="data/interdomain_rotation/APO/1/plumed.dattheta_rev_deg">The MATHEVAL action with label <b>theta_rev_deg</b> calculates the following quantities:<table  align="center" frame="void" width="95%" cellpadding="5%"><tr><td width="5%"><b> Quantity </b>  </td><td><b> Description </b> </td></tr><tr><td width="5%">theta_rev_deg.value</td><td>an arbitrary function</td></tr></table></span><b name="data/interdomain_rotation/APO/1/plumed.datuwall_z" onclick='showPath("data/interdomain_rotation/APO/1/plumed.dat","data/interdomain_rotation/APO/1/plumed.datuwall_z","data/interdomain_rotation/APO/1/plumed.datuwall_z","brown")'>uwall_z</b>: <span class="plumedtooltip" style="color:green">UPPER_WALLS<span class="right">Defines a wall for the value of one or more collective variables, <a href="https://www.plumed.org/doc-master/user-doc/html/UPPER_WALLS" style="color:green">More details</a><i></i></span></span> <span class="plumedtooltip">ARG<span class="right">the arguments on which the bias is acting<i></i></span></span>=<b name="data/interdomain_rotation/APO/1/plumed.datp_rmsd_twist_l0p1">p_rmsd_twist_l0p1.z</b> <span class="plumedtooltip">AT<span class="right">the positions of the wall<i></i></span></span>=2 <span class="plumedtooltip">KAPPA<span class="right">the force constant for the wall<i></i></span></span>=10000.0 <span class="plumedtooltip">EXP<span class="right"> the powers for the walls<i></i></span></span>=2

<span style="color:blue" class="comment">#  OPES: path + ionic lock  (all replicas) </span>
<span style="display:none;" id="data/interdomain_rotation/APO/1/plumed.datuwall_z">The UPPER_WALLS action with label <b>uwall_z</b> calculates the following quantities:<table  align="center" frame="void" width="95%" cellpadding="5%"><tr><td width="5%"><b> Quantity </b>  </td><td><b> Description </b> </td></tr><tr><td width="5%">uwall_z.bias</td><td>the instantaneous value of the bias potential</td></tr><tr><td width="5%">uwall_z.force2</td><td>the instantaneous value of the squared force due to this bias potential</td></tr></table></span><span class="plumedtooltip" style="color:green">OPES_METAD_EXPLORE<span class="right">On-the-fly probability enhanced sampling with well-tempered target distribution in exploreation mode. <a href="https://www.plumed.org/doc-master/user-doc/html/OPES_METAD_EXPLORE" style="color:green">More details</a><i></i></span></span> ...
  <span class="plumedtooltip">LABEL<span class="right">a label for the action so that its output can be referenced in the input to other actions<i></i></span></span>=<b name="data/interdomain_rotation/APO/1/plumed.datopes_main" onclick='showPath("data/interdomain_rotation/APO/1/plumed.dat","data/interdomain_rotation/APO/1/plumed.datopes_main","data/interdomain_rotation/APO/1/plumed.datopes_main","brown")'>opes_main</b>
  <span class="plumedtooltip">ARG<span class="right">the labels of the scalars on which the bias will act (a vector must have a single element, e<i></i></span></span>=<b name="data/interdomain_rotation/APO/1/plumed.datp_rmsd_twist_l0p1">p_rmsd_twist_l0p1.s</b>
  <span class="plumedtooltip">SIGMA<span class="right"> the initial widths of the kernels, divided by the square root of gamma<i></i></span></span>=0.01
  <span class="plumedtooltip">FILE<span class="right"> a file in which the list of all deposited kernels is stored<i></i></span></span>=Kernels_main.data
  <span class="plumedtooltip">STATE_RFILE<span class="right">read from this file the compressed kernels and all the info needed to RESTART the simulation<i></i></span></span>=compressed.Kernels_main
  <span class="plumedtooltip">STATE_WFILE<span class="right">write to this file the compressed kernels and all the info needed to RESTART the simulation<i></i></span></span>=compressed.Kernels_main
  <span class="plumedtooltip">PACE<span class="right">the frequency for kernel deposition<i></i></span></span>=50000 <span class="plumedtooltip">BARRIER<span class="right">the free energy barrier to be overcome<i></i></span></span>=50
... OPES_METAD_EXPLORE
<br/><span style="color:blue" class="comment">#  OPES: polar core  (replicas 1-4) </span>
<span style="display:none;" id="data/interdomain_rotation/APO/1/plumed.datopes_main">The OPES_METAD_EXPLORE action with label <b>opes_main</b> calculates the following quantities:<table  align="center" frame="void" width="95%" cellpadding="5%"><tr><td width="5%"><b> Quantity </b>  </td><td><b> Description </b> </td></tr><tr><td width="5%">opes_main.bias</td><td>the instantaneous value of the bias potential</td></tr><tr><td width="5%">opes_main.rct</td><td>estimate of c(t)</td></tr><tr><td width="5%">opes_main.zed</td><td>estimate of Z_n</td></tr><tr><td width="5%">opes_main.neff</td><td>effective sample size</td></tr><tr><td width="5%">opes_main.nker</td><td>total number of compressed kernels used to represent the bias</td></tr></table></span><span class="plumedtooltip" style="color:green">OPES_METAD_EXPLORE<span class="right">On-the-fly probability enhanced sampling with well-tempered target distribution in exploreation mode. <a href="https://www.plumed.org/doc-master/user-doc/html/OPES_METAD_EXPLORE" style="color:green">More details</a><i></i></span></span> ...
